[u4311 | 02:26:41.472-02:26:46.032 | DM] ... metal you had. It's what power, well, it's what holds the binding stone...

[u10173 | 05:36:20.444-05:36:23.084 | Kenzo] You know, but it's cleared now. There's nothing in there, and it's, uh, you know.
[u10174 | 05:36:23.344-05:36:23.624 | Wellby] But tainted by the-
[u10175 | 05:36:23.664-05:36:24.504 | Unknown] Ex- except for the, the, the-
[u10176 | 05:36:24.504-05:36:25.764 | Wellby] ... memory of an unspeakable evil
[u10177 | 05:36:25.984-05:36:32.384 | Kenzo] Yeah, but, but also the, you know, the divine forge that's there that can make, um, you know, really powerful weapons if you wanna do that, or artifacts,
[u10178 | 05:36:33.184-05:36:34.264 | Kenzo] if you're into artificing.
[u10179 | 05:36:35.184-05:36:35.804 | DM] Not really.
[u10180 | 05:36:35.924-05:36:37.244 | Seeker] There's, there's a throne. We took the throne.
[u10181 | 05:36:38.044-05:36:38.184 | DM] Wait.
[u10182 | 05:36:38.184-05:36:38.304 | Seeker] But-
[u10183 | 05:36:38.324-05:36:39.184 | DM] Where is the throne?
[u10184 | 05:36:39.784-05:36:42.204 | Unknown] Well, we took, we took it. [laughs]
[u10185 | 05:36:42.304-05:36:44.304 | DM] You're wearing the throne around your neck?
[u10186 | 05:36:44.904-05:36:45.784 | Unknown] Extradimensional.
[u10187 | 05:36:45.784-05:36:46.504 | Seeker] [laughs] Yeah.
[u10188 | 05:36:46.884-05:36:46.964 | DM] Ah.
[u10189 | 05:36:46.964-05:36:48.004 | Kenzo] Spaces, you know. Yeah.
[u10190 | 05:36:48.684-05:36:48.804 | DM] Yeah.
[u10191 | 05:36:48.804-05:36:49.544 | Unknown] We got a few tricks.
[u10192 | 05:36:49.584-05:36:49.924 | Seeker] Clever person.
[u10193 | 05:36:49.924-05:36:50.684 | Kenzo] Yeah, we're clever.
[u10194 | 05:36:50.904-05:36:51.104 | DM] Yeah.
[u10195 | 05:36:52.464-05:36:53.884 | Delwath] Yeah. Hey, do you know how to identify items?
[u10196 | 05:36:56.824-05:36:57.264 | DM] He, like,
[u10197 | 05:36:58.264-05:37:00.484 | DM] looks at his wand, looks at his staff.
[u10198 | 05:37:01.964-05:37:03.184 | DM] Fire?
[u10199 | 05:37:03.204-05:37:03.624 | DM] Ice?
[u10200 | 05:37:04.744-05:37:05.444 | DM] Mm, no.
[u10201 | 05:37:07.004-05:37:08.164 | Seeker] So you just got those two tricks?
[u10202 | 05:37:10.044-05:37:10.624 | DM] Stone.
[u10203 | 05:37:12.424-05:37:14.744 | Unknown] I can speak like the gods.
[u10204 | 05:37:14.744-05:37:15.444 | DM] [laughs]
[u10205 | 05:37:15.444-05:37:15.564 | Wellby] [laughs]
[u10206 | 05:37:15.604-05:37:16.724 | Unknown] That's, that's a good trick too.
[u10207 | 05:37:16.864-05:37:17.904 | Wellby] That's a, that's a good one.
[u10208 | 05:37:17.924-05:37:18.404 | Seeker] Yeah, it's very loud.
[u10209 | 05:37:18.584-05:37:18.664 | DM] Yeah.
[u10210 | 05:37:18.704-05:37:19.184 | Seeker] Very good.
[u10211 | 05:37:19.204-05:37:19.544 | Unknown] That's cool.
[u10212 | 05:37:20.024-05:37:20.344 | Seeker] Yeah.
[u10213 | 05:37:20.404-05:37:20.904 | DM] See, you, you are pretty good.
[u10214 | 05:37:20.924-05:37:22.264 | Kenzo] Try not to do that in our ears.
[u10215 | 05:37:22.284-05:37:22.884 | Unknown] That's kinda like, you know.
[u10216 | 05:37:22.884-05:37:23.964 | Seeker] You have a number of tricks, actually.
[u10217 | 05:37:23.984-05:37:25.364 | Wellby] Give, give us a heads up before you do that.
[u10218 | 05:37:25.404-05:37:26.084 | Kenzo] Yeah, that'd be nice.
[u10219 | 05:37:26.084-05:37:27.284 | Seeker] [laughs]
[u10220 | 05:37:27.344-05:37:27.884 | Kenzo] Respectful.
[u10221 | 05:37:29.164-05:37:32.364 | Kenzo] Guessing you haven't talked to anybody in, like, 17 years or whatever since we got here, so.
[u10222 | 05:37:32.424-05:37:33.824 | DM] Oh, I talked to a few people.
[u10223 | 05:37:34.384-05:37:35.224 | Unknown] Really?
[u10224 | 05:37:35.304-05:37:37.104 | DM] Yeah, a couple dragonets. How do you think I knew about-
[u10225 | 05:37:37.144-05:37:38.384 | Kenzo] Oh, the dragonets. Okay, yeah.
[u10226 | 05:37:38.404-05:37:39.424 | Kenzo] But no, no, humanoids.
[u10227 | 05:37:40.704-05:37:40.884 | DM] Oh, oh.
[u10228 | 05:37:40.924-05:37:41.144 | Unknown] Ah.
[u10229 | 05:37:41.144-05:37:42.344 | DM] That's what you meant by people.
[u10230 | 05:37:42.504-05:37:42.584 | Seeker] I see.
[u10231 | 05:37:42.584-05:37:43.504 | DM] None of them, no.
[u10232 | 05:37:43.504-05:37:44.064 | Unknown] Kenzo.
[u10233 | 05:37:44.064-05:37:45.164 | Wellby] [laughs]
[u10234 | 05:37:45.164-05:37:45.544 | DM] [laughs]
[u10235 | 05:37:45.544-05:37:46.804 | Unknown] People, people.
[u10236 | 05:37:46.804-05:37:47.924 | Unknown] I'm just using the word people.
[u10237 | 05:37:49.064-05:37:49.624 | Seeker] Uh-huh.
[u10238 | 05:37:49.624-05:37:50.204 | DM] [laughs]
[u10239 | 05:37:50.204-05:37:51.664 | Seeker] Bipedal, normally.
[u10240 | 05:37:52.544-05:37:53.744 | Delwath] Which method you like for that one?
[u10241 | 05:37:53.744-05:37:56.724 | Delwath] Two legs, that somehow makes people more worthy of, uh-
[u10242 | 05:37:56.744-05:37:59.184 | Kenzo] No, that's not... No, that, that's your perspective, elf.
[u10243 | 05:37:59.604-05:38:02.624 | Kenzo] You know, I'm, I'm a, I'm all, I'm a, you know, all consciousness
[u10244 | 05:38:04.124-05:38:06.104 | Kenzo] are part of the same large unity, so, you know.
[u10245 | 05:38:07.724-05:38:08.164 | Unknown] Okay.
[u10246 | 05:38:09.104-05:38:10.304 | DM] So you're here to sleep again?
[u10247 | 05:38:11.184-05:38:11.744 | Kenzo] That's all right.
[u10248 | 05:38:13.084-05:38:13.864 | DM] Yeah, why not?
[u10249 | 05:38:15.284-05:38:16.004 | Seeker] It's been, uh,
[u10250 | 05:38:16.784-05:38:17.404 | Seeker] it's been a long day.
[u10251 | 05:38:17.564-05:38:18.163 | Wellby] It's been a day.
[u10252 | 05:38:18.503-05:38:21.164 | Unknown] Yeah, it's been a day.
[u10253 | 05:38:21.284-05:38:22.884 | DM] Uh-huh. [yawns] Okay.
[u10254 | 05:38:24.024-05:38:24.824 | DM] Good seeing you.
[u10255 | 05:38:25.424-05:38:27.144 | Kenzo] Yeah, glad you think so. We think so too.
[u10256 | 05:38:27.204-05:38:28.304 | DM] I didn't think you'd come back.
[u10257 | 05:38:28.964-05:38:29.424 | Kenzo] Alive?
[u10258 | 05:38:31.684-05:38:32.184 | DM] Yeah.
[u10259 | 05:38:32.184-05:38:33.464 | Seeker] We had some dicey moments there.
[u10260 | 05:38:35.964-05:38:37.204 | Wellby] I think we did pretty much great.
[u10261 | 05:38:37.504-05:38:38.544 | Seeker] Yeah, we s- we should-
[u10262 | 05:38:38.584-05:38:40.964 | Delwath] I mean, we only got, you know, we got, like, five crits.
[u10263 | 05:38:41.604-05:38:42.404 | Delwath] Of course we did great.
